The distance matrix is\n// therefore distributed by contiguous blocks of rows over the MPI ranks; every rank owns\n// rows [rowBegin, rowEnd) of both the distance and the path matrix.\n\n// Row distribution: block distribution with the remainder spread over the first ranks.\ninline size_t rowBeginOf(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);\n    return r * base + std::min(r, rem);\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const size_t rowBegin, const size_t numLocalRows,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    // Reproduce the sequential random stream: consume the values belonging to the rows\n    // owned by lower ranks, then generate the local ones.\n    const size_t skip = rowBegin * numNodes;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < skip; ++i) {\n        (void)rand_r(&seed);\n    }\n\n    const size_t localElements = numLocalRows * numNodes;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < localElements; ++i) {\n        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numLocalRows; ++i) {\n        dist[i * numNodes + (rowBegin + i)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,\n                          const size_t rowBegin, const size_t numLocalRows) {\n    // The sequential initialization assigns path[row][col] = row for every entry.\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numLocalRows; ++i) {\n        std::fill_n(path.data() + i * numNodes, numNodes, static_cast<unsigned int>(rowBegin + i));\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax one row against the pivot row. Branchless so that the compiler can vectorize the\n// conditional distance/path update into blended stores.\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict distRow, unsigned int* __restrict pathRow,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict pivotRow, const unsigned int distIK,\n                            const unsigned int k, const size_t numNodes) noexcept {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivotRow[j];\n        const bool update = newDist < distRow[j];\n        distRow[j] = update ? newDist : distRow[j];\n        pathRow[j] = update ? k : pathRow[j];\n    }\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const size_t numNodes,\n                   const size_t rowBegin,\n                   const size_t numLocalRows,\n                   const std::vector<int>& rowOwner,\n                   MPI_Comm comm) {\n    // Distributed Floyd-Warshall: for every intermediate node k all ranks need the pivot\n    // row k, which is broadcast by its owner. The broadcast of the next pivot row is\n    // overlapped with the computation of the current step: the owner of row k+1 relaxes\n    // that row first, then starts a non-blocking broadcast, and only afterwards processes\n    // its remaining rows.\n    const size_t rowEnd = rowBegin + numLocalRows;\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotBuf(numNodes ? 2 * numNodes : 2);\n    unsigned int* pivot = pivotBuf.data();\n    unsigned int* pivotNext = pivotBuf.data() + numNodes;\n\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Prime the pipeline with pivot row 0.\n    if (rowOwner[0] == static_cast<int>(rowBegin < rowEnd ? rowOwner[rowBegin] : -1) &&\n        rowBegin <= 0 && 0 < rowEnd) {\n        std::memcpy(pivot, dist.data() + (0 - rowBegin) * numNodes, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n    }\n    MPI_Bcast(pivot, static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED, rowOwner[0], comm);\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            // Row k is a fixed point of its own iteration (dist[k][k] == 0), and the next\n            // pivot row has already been processed above.\n            if (i == k || i == skipRow) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            unsigned int* distRow = dist.data() + (i - rowBegin) * numNodes;\n            unsigned int* pathRow = path.data() + (i - rowBegin) * numNodes;\n            relaxRow(distRow, pathRow, pivot, distRow[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), numNodes);\n        }\n\n        if (kNext < numNodes) {\n            MPI_Wait(&req, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n            std::swap(pivot, pivotNext);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    const bool isRoot = (rank == 0);\n\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (isRoot) {\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (isRoot) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", numRanks);\n    }\n\n    // Row distribution over the ranks\n    const size_t rowBegin = rowBeginOf(numNodes, rank, numRanks);\n    const size_t rowEnd = rowBeginOf(numNodes, rank + 1, numRanks);\n    const size_t numLocalRows = rowEnd - rowBegin;\n\n    std::vector<int> rowOwner(numNodes);\n    std::vector<int> sendCounts(numRanks);\n    std::vector<int> displs(numRanks);\n    for The distance matrix is\n// therefore distributed by contiguous blocks of rows over the MPI ranks; every rank owns\n// rows [rowBegin, rowEnd) of both the distance and the path matrix.\n\n// Row distribution: block distribution with the remainder spread over the first ranks.\ninline size_t rowBeginOf(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);\n    return r * base + std::min(r, rem);\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const size_t rowBegin, const size_t numLocalRows,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    // Reproduce the sequential random stream: consume the values belonging to the rows\n    // owned by lower ranks, then generate the local ones.\n    const size_t skip = rowBegin * numNodes;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < skip; ++i) {\n        (void)rand_r(&seed);\n    }\n\n    const size_t localElements = numLocalRows * numNodes;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < localElements; ++i) {\n        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numLocalRows; ++i) {\n        dist[i * numNodes + (rowBegin + i)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,\n                          const size_t rowBegin, const size_t numLocalRows) {\n    // The sequential initialization assigns path[row][col] = row for every entry.\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numLocalRows; ++i) {\n        std::fill_n(path.data() + i * numNodes, numNodes, static_cast<unsigned int>(rowBegin + i));\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax one row against the pivot row. Branchless so that the compiler can vectorize the\n// conditional distance/path update into blended stores.\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict distRow, unsigned int* __restrict pathRow,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict pivotRow, const unsigned int distIK,\n                            const unsigned int k, const size_t numNodes) noexcept {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivotRow[j];\n        const bool update = newDist < distRow[j];\n        distRow[j] = update ? newDist : distRow[j];\n        pathRow[j] = update ? k : pathRow[j];\n    }\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const size_t numNodes,\n                   const size_t rowBegin,\n                   const size_t numLocalRows,\n                   const std::vector<int>& rowOwner,\n                   MPI_Comm comm) {\n    // Distributed Floyd-Warshall: for every intermediate node k all ranks need the pivot\n    // row k, which is broadcast by its owner. The broadcast of the next pivot row is\n    // overlapped with the computation of the current step: the owner of row k+1 relaxes\n    // that row first, then starts a non-blocking broadcast, and only afterwards processes\n    // its remaining rows.\n    const size_t rowEnd = rowBegin + numLocalRows;\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotBuf(numNodes ? 2 * numNodes : 2);\n    unsigned int* pivot = pivotBuf.data();\n    unsigned int* pivotNext = pivotBuf.data() + numNodes;\n\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Prime the pipeline with pivot row 0.\n    if (rowOwner[0] == static_cast<int>(rowBegin < rowEnd ? rowOwner[rowBegin] : -1) &&\n        rowBegin <= 0 && 0 < rowEnd) {\n        std::memcpy(pivot, dist.data() + (0 - rowBegin) * numNodes, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n    }\n    MPI_Bcast(pivot, static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED, rowOwner[0], comm);\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n        const size_t kNext = k + 1;\n        MPI_Request req = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;\n        size_t skipRow = numNodes; // local row already handled below (none by default)\n\n        if (kNext < numNodes) {\n            if (kNext >= rowBegin && kNext < rowEnd) {\n                // Finish the next pivot row first so that it can be sent right away.\n                unsigned int* distRow = dist.data() + (kNext - rowBegin) * numNodes;\n                unsigned int* pathRow = path.data() + (kNext - rowBegin) * numNodes;\n                relaxRow(distRow, pathRow, pivot, distRow[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), numNodes);\n                std::memcpy(pivotNext, distRow, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n                skipRow = kNext;\n            }\n            MPI_Ibcast(pivotNext, static_cast<int>(numNodes), MPI_UNSIGNED, rowOwner[kNext], comm, &req);\n        }\n\n        for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n            // Row k is a fixed point of its own iteration (dist[k][k] == 0), and the next\n            // pivot row has already been processed above.\n            if (i == k || i == skipRow) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            unsigned int* distRow = dist.data() + (i - rowBegin) * numNodes;\n            unsigned int* pathRow = path.data() + (i - rowBegin) * numNodes;\n            relaxRow(distRow, pathRow, pivot, distRow[k], static_cast<unsigned int>(k), numNodes);\n        }\n\n        if (kNext < numNodes) {\n            MPI_Wait(&req, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n            std::swap(pivot, pivotNext);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    const bool isRoot = (rank == 0);\n\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (isRoot) {\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (isRoot) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: numRanks);\n    const size_t numLocalRows = rowEnd - rowBegin;\n\n    std::vector<int> rowOwner(numNodes);\n    std::vector<int> sendCounts(numRanks);\n    std::vector<int> displs(numRanks);\n    for static_cast<int>((end - begin) * numNodes);\n        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(begin * numNodes);\n        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            rowOwner[i] = r;\n        }\n    }\n\n        printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    }\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, rowBegin, numLocalRows, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes, rowBegin, numLocalRows);\n\n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    }\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start =   auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    long long localMs = static_cast<long long>(duration.count());\n    long long elapsedMs = localMs;\n    MPI_Reduce(&localMs, &elapsedMs, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %lld ms\\n\", elapsedMs);\n\n        // Calculate operations per second\n        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n        double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n        double gflops = ops / (elapsedMs / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        // Collect the full distance matrix on the root for output and printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n                } else {\n                    printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                    exitCode = 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n Branchless so that the compiler can vectorize the\n67\t// conditional distance/path update into blended stores.\n68\tstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict distRow, unsigned int* __restrict pathRow,\n69\t                            const unsigned int* __restrict pivotRow, const unsigned int distIK,\n70\t                            const unsigned int k, const size_t numNodes) noexcept {\n71\t    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n72\t        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + pivotRow[j];\n73\t        const bool update = newDist < distRow[j];\n74\t        distRow[j] = update ? newDist : distRow[j];\n75\t        pathRow[j] = update ? k : pathRow[j];\n76\t    }\n77\t}\n78\t\n79\tvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n80\t                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n81\t                   const size_t numNodes,\n82\t                   const size_t rowBegin,\n83\t                   const size_t numLocalRows,\n84\t                   const std::vector<int>& rowOwner,\n85\t                   MPI_Comm comm) {\n86\t    // Distributed Floyd-Warshall: for every intermediate node k all ranks need the pivot\n87\t    // row k, which is broadcast by its owner. The pivot rows of the\n    // k-loop are handled in panels: the owner of a panel resolves it locally and broadcasts\n    // the snapshots of its pivot rows, then every rank applies the whole panel to its own\n    // rows. \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;\nconstexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;\n\n// Index calculation for flattened 2D array\ninline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    return j * n + i;\n}\n\n// The flattened layout is row-major with respect to the algorithm's access pattern:\n// dist[idx2(j, i, The distance matrix is\n// therefore distributed by contiguous blocks of rows over the MPI ranks; every rank owns\n// rows [rowBegin, rowEnd) of both the distance and the path matrix.\n\n// Row distribution: block distribution with the remainder spread over the first ranks.\ninline size_t rowBeginOf(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);\n    return r * base + std::min(r, rem);\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const size_t rowBegin, const size_t numLocalRows,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    // Reproduce the sequential random stream: consume the values belonging to the rows\n    // owned by lower ranks, then generate the local ones.\n    const size_t skip = rowBegin * numNodes;\n    for (size_t i = 0; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CeevisMWKq1sypJAS6MUr","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. Both files updated in place; no new files (my scratch build/verification artifacts were kept in `/tmp` and removed).\n\n## What changed\n\n`floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp` — unconditional MPI parallelization:\n\n**Decomposition.** The flat layout `dist[idx2(j,i,n)] == dist[i*n+j]` is row-major in the algorithm's own access pattern, so the matrix is distributed as contiguous **block rows**: each rank allocates and owns only its `[rowBegin, rowEnd)` slice of both `dist` and `path` (memory scales with 1/P). Remainder rows are spread over the first ranks.\n\n**Initialization** is done locally without communication: each rank replays the sequential `rand_r` stream, discarding the values belonging to lower ranks, so the generated graph is identical to the original for any rank count. The path matrix init collapses to `path[row][*] = row`, which is what the original double loop computes.\n\n**Kernel.** Pivot rows are communicated and applied in **panels** of consecutive `k` values, each panel owned by a single rank (panel boundaries are aligned to rank boundaries):\n1. the owner resolves the panel against itself, snapshotting each pivot row at the moment the sequential algorithm would use it;\n2. the panel is broadcast once (`kb × n` values) instead of one broadcast per `k`, cutting message count by up to 16×;\n3. every rank applies the whole panel to its local rows, blocked over rows so `dist`/`path` rows stay in cache across several pivot rows.\n\nThe next panel's broadcast is overlapped with the current panel's computation via `MPI_Ibcast`: the next owner brings its panel rows up to date and prepares them first, then posts the broadcast, then does its bulk work. Panel size is chosen as `rowsPerRank/16` (capped at 64) — the owner's panel resolution sits on the critical path, so it must stay small relative to the per-rank row count; this was tuned empirically (a fixed 64 cost ~40% at 128 ranks).\n\nThe inner relaxation is branchless so GCC if-converts it to `vpaddd`/`vpminud`/`vpblendvb` — verified in the disassembly.\n\n**Output.** Rank 0 does all printing; timing is `MPI_Barrier` + max-reduce over ranks; `dist` is gathered to rank 0 with `MPI_Gatherv` only when `-r`/`-v` is given, and validation/exit code is broadcast so all ranks agree.\n\n`floydwarshall/CMakeLists.txt` — `find_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)` + link `MPI::MPI_CXX`. Executable name, flags, and standard unchanged.\n\n## Verification\n\nOrdering-independence is exact, not approximate: rows are independent within a `k` step and every row still sees pivot rows in ascending `k` order, so results are bit-identical. Checked against a rebuilt copy of the original serial code — both the `DistanceMatrix` hash **and** a full `path`-matrix hash match for n ∈ {1, 2, 5, 7, 129, 137, 300, 512, 1000, 1024, 2048} × P ∈ {1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 8, 16, 33, 64, 128}, including n < P. `-v` passes, and `-n 0`, `-h`, and unknown-option paths behave as before (all ranks finalize cleanly).\n\n## Performance (2× AMD EPYC 7763, 128 cores, n = 4096)\n\n| ranks | 1 | 8 | 16 | 32 | 64 | 128 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| GOPS | 2.79 | 21.6 | 41.5 | 78 | 139 | 138–159 |\n\nSingle-rank is ~6% faster than the original serial code (2.79 vs 2.63 GOPS) thanks to the cache blocking; 128 ranks give ~50–57× speedup over one rank. Run-to-run variance at 64/128 ranks is significant on this shared machine (the 109–159 GOPS spread), driven by NUMA placement — `--map-by core`/`numa` binding matters more than any remaining code-level tuning."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":776,"cache_read_input_tokens":85253,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":776},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"99f0929d-e213-4741-bc7f-066ed6f51dc6","uuid":"e0072cce-b91e-4743-9188-a444142363a4","timestamp":"2026-09-02T16:27:48.231Z","request_id":"req_011CeevirTRo2UbBTk6Nh3Va"}
